### [DIVISION IX]{.HLabel2}[Administration and Enforcement]{.HTitleText2} {#h-153609 .Subheading}

#### [Disclosure of Information]{.HTitleText3} {#h-153610 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[119]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-119 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section,

  [[delineation well]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[delineation well]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a well that is so
      located in relation to another well penetrating an accumulation of
      petroleum that there is a reasonable expectation that another
      portion of that accumulation will be penetrated by the
      first-mentioned well and that the drilling is necessary in order
      to determine the commercial value of the accumulation; ([puits de
      délimitation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[development well]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[development well]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a well that is so
      located in relation to another well penetrating an accumulation of
      petroleum that it is considered to be a well or part of a well
      drilled for the purpose of production or observation or for the
      injection or disposal of fluid into or from the
      accumulation; ([puits d'exploitation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[engineering research or feasibility study]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[engineering research or feasibility
      study]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes work undertaken to facilitate
      the design or to analyse the viability of engineering technology,
      systems or schemes to be used, in the offshore area, in the
      exploration for or the development, production or transportation
      of petroleum or in offshore renewable energy
      projects; ([recherches ou études techniques]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[environmental study]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[environmental study]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means work pertaining
      to the measurement or statistical evaluation of the physical,
      chemical and biological elements of the lands, oceans or coastal
      zones, including winds, waves, tides, currents, precipitation, ice
      cover and movement, icebergs, pollution effects, flora and fauna
      both onshore and offshore, human activity and habitation and any
      related matters; ([études de l'environnement]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[experimental project]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[experimental project]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means work or
      activity involving the utilization of methods or equipment that
      are untried or unproven; ([opération
      expérimentale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[exploratory well]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[exploratory well]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a well drilled on a
      geological feature on which a significant discovery has not been
      made; ([puits d'exploration]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[geological work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[geological work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means work, in the field
      or laboratory, involving the collection, examination, processing
      or other analysis of lithological, paleontological or geochemical
      materials recovered from the seabed or subsoil of any portion of
      the offshore area and includes the analysis and interpretation of
      mechanical well logs; ([travaux de géologie]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[geophysical work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[geophysical work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means work involving the
      indirect measurement of the physical properties of rocks in order
      to determine the depth, thickness, structural configuration or
      history of deposition thereof and includes the processing,
      analysis and interpretation of material or data obtained from such
      work; ([travaux de géophysique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[geotechnical work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[geotechnical work]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means work, in the field
      or laboratory, undertaken to determine the physical properties of
      materials recovered from the seabed or subsoil of any portion of
      the offshore area; ([travaux de géotechnique]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[well site seabed survey]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[well site seabed survey]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a survey
      pertaining to the nature of the seabed or subsoil of any portion
      of the offshore area in the area of the proposed drilling site in
      respect of a well and to the conditions of those portions of the
      offshore area that may affect the safety or efficiency of drilling
      operations; ([levé marin]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[well termination date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[well termination date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the date on
      which a well has been abandoned, completed or suspended in
      accordance with any applicable regulations respecting the drilling
      for petroleum made under Part III. ([date d'abandon du
      puits]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Privilege

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 18 and this section, information
  or documentation provided for the purposes of this Part or Part III or
  any regulation made under either Part, whether or not such information
  or documentation is required to be provided under either Part or any
  regulation made thereunder, is privileged and shall not knowingly be
  disclosed without the consent in writing of the person who provided it
  except for the purposes of the administration or enforcement of either
  Part or for the purposes of legal proceedings relating to such
  administration or enforcement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person shall be required to produce or give
  evidence relating to any information or documentation that is
  privileged under subsection (2) in connection with any legal
  proceedings, other than proceedings relating to the administration or
  enforcement of this Part or Part III.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration of documents

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, this section does not apply to
  a document that has been registered under Division VIII.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information that may be disclosed

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) does not apply to the following
  classes of information or documentation obtained as a result of
  carrying on a work or activity that is authorized under Part III,
  namely, information or documentation in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an exploratory well, where the information or
    documentation is obtained as a direct result of drilling the well
    and if two years have passed since the well termination date of that
    well;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a delineation well, where the information or
    documentation is obtained as a direct result of drilling the well
    and if the later of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} two years since the well termination date of the
      relevant exploratory well, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} ninety days since the well termination date of
      the delineation well,

    have passed;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a development well, where the information or
    documentation is obtained as a direct result of drilling the well
    and if the later of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} two years since the well termination date of the
      relevant exploratory well, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} sixty days since the well termination date of
      the development well,

    have passed;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} geological work or geophysical work performed on or
    in relation to any portion of the offshore area,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a well site seabed survey where
      the well has been drilled, after the expiration of the period
      referred to in paragraph (a) or the later period referred to in
      subparagraph (b)(i) or (ii) or (c)(i) or (ii), according to
      whether paragraph (a), (b) or (c) is applicable in respect of that
      well,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case relating to a
      petroleum-related work or activity, after the end of five years
      following the date of completion of the work, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in any case relating to an offshore renewable
      energy project, after the end of the time set out by regulations
      or, in the absence of regulations, three years following the date
      of completion of the geological or geophysical work;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any engineering research or feasibility study or
    experimental project, including geotechnical work, carried out on or
    in relation to any portion of the offshore area,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} where it relates to a well and the well has been
      drilled, after the expiration of the period referred to in
      paragraph (a) or the later period referred to in subparagraph
      (b)(i) or (ii) or (c)(i) or (ii), according to whether paragraph
      (a), (b) or (c) is applicable in respect of that well,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case relating to a
      petroleum-related work or activity, after the end of five years
      following the date of completion of the research, study or
      experimental project or after the reversion of that portion of the
      offshore area to Crown reserve areas, whichever occurs first, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in any case relating to an offshore renewable
      energy project, after the earlier of:

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the end of the period set out by regulations
        or, in the absence of regulations, three years following the
        date of completion of the research, study or experimental
        project, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} the reversion of that portion of the offshore
        area to Crown reserve areas;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} any contingency plan formulated in respect of
    emergencies arising as a result of any work or activity authorized
    under Part III;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} diving work, weather observation or the status of
    operational activities or of the development of or production from a
    pool or field;

  - [(g.1)]{.lawlabel} accidents, incidents or petroleum spills, to the
    extent necessary to permit a person or body to produce and to
    distribute or publish a report for the administration of this Act in
    respect of the accident, incident or spill;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} any study funded from an account established under
    subsection 76(1) of the [Canada Petroleum Resources
    Act](/eng/acts/C-8.5), if the study has been completed; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an environmental study, other than a study referred
    to in paragraph (h),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} where it relates to a well and the well has been
      drilled, after the expiration of the period referred to in
      paragraph (a) or the later period referred to in subparagraph
      (b)(i) or (ii) or (c)(i) or (ii), according to whether paragraph
      (a), (b) or (c) is applicable in respect of that well,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case relating to a
      petroleum-related work or activity, if five years have passed
      since the completion of the study, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in any case relating to an offshore renewable
      energy project, if the period set out by regulations has passed
      since the completion of the study or, in the absence of
      regulations, if three years have passed since the completion of
      that study.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure --- governments and agencies

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Regulator may disclose any information or
  documentation that it obtains under this Part or Part III --- to
  officials of the Government of Canada, the Government of the Province
  or any other provincial government, or a foreign government or to the
  representatives of any of their agencies --- for the purposes of a
  federal, provincial or foreign law, as the case may be, that deals
  primarily with a petroleum-related work or activity, including the
  exploration for and the management, administration and exploitation of
  petroleum resources, or with an offshore renewable energy project, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the government or agency undertakes to keep the
    information or documentation confidential and not to disclose it
    without the Regulator's written consent;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the information and documentation is disclosed in
    accordance with any conditions agreed to by the Regulator and the
    government or agency; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of disclosure to a foreign government
    or agency, the Federal Minister and Provincial Minister consent in
    writing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure --- Minister

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The Regulator may disclose to the Federal Minister
  and Provincial Minister the information or documentation that it has
  disclosed or intends to disclose under subsection (6), but the Federal
  Minister and the Provincial Minister are not to further disclose that
  information or documentation unless the Regulator consents in writing
  to that disclosure or the Federal Minister or the Provincial Minister
  is required by an Act of Parliament or an Act of the Legislature of
  the Province, as the case may be, to disclose that information or
  documentation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consent

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (6)(a) and subsection
  (7), the Regulator may consent to the further disclosure of
  information or documentation only if the Regulator itself is
  authorized under this section to disclose it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Applicant and proposed work or activity

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) does not apply in respect of
  information regarding the applicant for an operating licence or
  authorization under subsection 138(1) or authorization under
  subsection 138.01(1) or in respect of the scope, purpose, location,
  timing and nature of the proposed work or activity for which the
  licence or authorization is sought.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Public notice

  [(9.1)]{.lawlabel} The Regulator shall make public a summary of the
  information referred to in subsection (9) in respect of a work or
  activity for which the Impact Assessment Agency of Canada decided
  under section 16 of the [Impact Assessment Act](/eng/acts/I-2.75) that
  an impact assessment is not required or which is excluded under
  section 112.1 of that Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Public hearing

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (2) does not apply in respect of
  information or documentation provided for the purposes of a public
  hearing conducted under section 44.1.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Safety or environmental protection

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 119.1, the Regulator may
  disclose, including for the purposes of the [Impact Assessment
  Act](/eng/acts/I-2.75), all or part of any information or
  documentation related to safety or environmental protection that is
  provided in relation to an application for an operating licence or
  authorization under subsection 138(1) or authorization under
  subsection 138.01(1), or to an operating licence or authorization that
  is issued under one of those subsections or provided in accordance
  with any regulations made under this Part or Part III. The Regulator
  is not, however, permitted to disclose information or documentation if
  it is satisfied that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} disclosure of it could reasonably be expected to
    result in a material loss or gain to a person, or to prejudice their
    competitive position, and the potential harm resulting from the
    disclosure outweighs the public interest in making the disclosure;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is financial, commercial, scientific or
    technical information or documentation that is confidential and has
    been consistently treated as such by a person who would be directly
    affected by its disclosure, and for which the person's interest in
    confidentiality outweighs the public interest in its disclosure; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} there is a real and substantial risk that
    disclosure of it will impair the security of
    [[pipelines]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in section 135,
    installations, facilities, vessels, aircraft, or systems, including
    computer or communication systems, used for any work or activity in
    respect of which this Act applies --- or methods employed to protect
    them --- and the need to prevent its disclosure outweighs the public
    interest in its disclosure.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(12)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (9) to (11) do not apply in respect of
  information or documentation described in paragraphs (5)(a) to (e) and
  (i).

::: HistoricalNote

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice --- subsection 119(11)

- **[[119.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-119.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the Regulator intends to disclose
  any information or documentation under subsection 119(11), the
  Regulator shall make every reasonable effort to give the person who
  provided it written notice of the Regulator's intention to disclose
  it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Waiver of notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Any person to whom a notice is required to be given
  under subsection (1) may waive the requirement, and if they have
  consented to the disclosure they are deemed to have waived the
  requirement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A notice given under subsection (1) shall include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the Regulator intends to disclose
    information or documentation under subsection 119(11);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a description of the information or documentation
    that was provided by the person to whom the notice is given; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the person may, within 20 days
    after the day on which the notice is given, make written
    representations to the Regulator as to why the information or
    documentation, or a portion of it, should not be disclosed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a notice is given to a person under subsection
  (1), the Regulator shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} give the person the opportunity to make, within 20
    days after the day on which the notice is given, written
    representations to the Regulator as to why the information or
    documentation, or a portion of it, should not be disclosed; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} after the person has had the opportunity to make
    representations, but no later than 30 days after the day on which
    the notice is given, make a decision as to whether or not to
    disclose the information or documentation and give written notice of
    the decision to the person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of notice of decision to
  disclose

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A notice given under paragraph (4)(b) of a decision
  to disclose information or documentation shall include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a statement that the person to whom the notice is
    given may request a review of the decision under subsection (7)
    within 20 days after the day on which the notice is given; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a statement that if no review is requested under
    subsection (7) within 20 days after the day on which the notice is
    given, the Regulator shall disclose the information or
    documentation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure of information or
  documentation

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If, under paragraph (4)(b), the Regulator decides to
  disclose the information or documentation, the Regulator shall
  disclose it on the expiry of 20 days after the day on which a notice
  is given under that paragraph, unless a review of the decision is
  requested under subsection (7).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Any person to whom the Regulator is required under
  paragraph (4)(b) to give a notice of a decision to disclose
  information or documentation may, within 20 days after the day on
  which the notice is given, apply to the Supreme Court of Newfoundland
  and Labrador for a review of the decision.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hearing in summary way

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} An application made under subsection (7) shall be
  heard and determined in a summary way in accordance with any
  applicable rules of practice and procedure of that Court.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Court to take precautions against
  disclosing

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} In any proceedings arising from an application under
  subsection (7), the Supreme Court of Newfoundland and Labrador shall
  take every reasonable precaution, including, when appropriate,
  conducting hearings [in camera]{.otherLang lang="la"}, to avoid the
  disclosure by the Court or any person of any information or
  documentation that, under this Act, is privileged or is not to be
  disclosed.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 47
- [2024, c. 20, s. 48](#1491197-1529747){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491197-1529747.html"}
- [2024, c. 20, s. 101](#1491355-1530761){.wb-lbx

#### [Arbitration]{.HTitleText3} {#h-153705 .Subheading}

**[[120]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-120 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 1992, c.
35, s. 49\]]{.Repealed}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Operating agreements

- **[[121]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-121
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where a dispute of a prescribed
  class arises between two or more interest holders of an interest in
  respect of any operations conducted in carrying out a work or activity
  in the offshore area authorized under Part III and an operating
  agreement or other similar arrangement that extends to such work or
  activity is not in force or was made prior to March 5, 1982, the
  matters in dispute may, by order of the Regulator, be submitted to
  arbitration conducted in accordance with the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) applies only in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} interests in force on March 5, 1982 in relation to
    any portion of the offshore area; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} interests immediately succeeding the interests
    referred to in paragraph (a) in relation to that portion of the
    offshore area where that portion of the offshore area was not a
    Crown reserve area on the expiration of the interests referred to in
    paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Arbitration order

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An order of an arbitrator made pursuant to
  arbitration under subsection (1) is binding on all interest holders
  specified in the order from the date specified in the order, and the
  terms and conditions of the order are deemed to be terms and
  conditions of the interest to which the matters relate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1987, c. 3, s. 121
- [2024, c. 20, s. 101](#1491355-1530771){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491355-1530771.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-121-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 121"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[122]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-122
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 7, the Governor
  in Council may make regulations for carrying out the purposes and
  provisions of section 121 and, without restricting the generality of
  the foregoing, may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} governing arbitration and the making of arbitration
    orders;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the classes of disputes that may be
    submitted to arbitration; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} governing appeals from and enforcement of
    arbitration orders.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of regulations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Regulations made under subsection (1) may apply
  generally to the offshore area or any portion thereof.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1987, c. 3, s. 122
- 1992, c. 35, s. 50
:::

#### [Cancellation of Rights]{.HTitleText3} {#h-153726 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to comply

- **[[123]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-123 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the Regulator has reason to believe that an interest owner or holder
  is failing or has failed to meet any requirement of this Part or Part
  III or III.1 or any regulation made under any of those Parts, the
  Regulator may give notice to that interest owner or holder requiring
  compliance with the requirement within 90 days after the day on which
  the notice is given or within any longer period that the Regulator
  considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Default --- petroleum

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite anything in this Part but subject to sections
  31 to 40 and subsection 123(3), if the interest owner or holder of a
  petroleum-related interest fails to comply with a notice under
  subsection (1) within the period specified in the notice and the
  Regulator considers that the failure to comply warrants cancellation
  of the interest of the interest owner or holder of or any share in the
  interest held by the holder with respect to a portion only of the
  offshore area subject to the interest, the Regulator may, by order
  subject to section 124, cancel that interest or share, in which case
  the portions of the offshore area under that interest or share become
  Crown reserve areas.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of section 124

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Regulator's order to cancel an exploration
  licence, significant discovery licence or production licence under
  subsection (2) is not subject to section 124 if the notice under
  subsection (1) relates to a failure to meet any of the terms and
  conditions contained in that licence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Default --- offshore renewable energy

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Despite anything in this Part but subject to sections
  40.1 to 40.3, if the interest owner or holder of a submerged land
  licence fails to comply with a notice under subsection (1) within the
  period specified in the notice and the Regulator considers that the
  failure to comply warrants cancellation of the licence of the interest
  owner or holder or any share in the licence held by the holder, the
  Regulator may, by order, cancel that licence or share, in which case
  the portions of the offshore area under that licence or share become
  Crown reserve areas.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1987, c. 3, s. 123
- 2014, c. 13, s. 20
- [2024, c. 20, s. 49](#1491202-1529752){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491202-1529752.html"}
- [2024, c. 20, s. 101](#1491355-1530773){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491355-1530773.html"}
:::

Version](section-123-20141231.html "Link to previous version of section 123"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Hearings and Judicial Review]{.HTitleText3} {#h-153734 .Subheading}

Definition of [[Committee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

- **[[124]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-124 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, [[Committee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Oil and Gas
  Committee established by Part III.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Regulator shall, not less than thirty days before
  making any order or decision or taking any action in respect of which
  it is expressly stated in this Part to be subject to this section,
  give notice in writing to the persons the Regulator considers to be
  directly affected by the proposed order, decision or action.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request for hearing

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Any person receiving a notice under subsection (2)
  may, in writing, request a hearing within the thirty day period
  referred to in that subsection and, on receipt of such a request, the
  Regulator shall direct the Committee to appoint a time and place for a
  hearing and give notice thereof to the person who requested the
  hearing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hearing

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Any person requesting a hearing under subsection (3)
  may make representations and introduce witnesses and documents at the
  hearing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of Committee

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of a hearing requested under
  subsection (3), the Committee has, regarding the attendance, swearing
  and examination of witnesses and the production and inspection of
  documents, all such powers, rights and privileges as are vested in a
  superior court of record.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recommendations of Committee

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} On the conclusion of the hearing, the Committee shall
  submit to the Regulator its recommendations concerning the proposed
  order, decision or action of the Regulator, together with the evidence
  and other material that was before the Committee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order of Regulator

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Before making any order or decision or taking any
  action in respect of which a hearing has been held, the Regulator
  shall consider the recommendations of the Committee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of order and reasons

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} If an order, decision or action referred to in
  subsection (2) is made or taken, the Regulator shall notify the person
  who requested a hearing in respect of the order, decision or action
  under subsection (3) and, on request by that person, publish or make
  available to that person the reasons for the order, decision or
  action.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effective date of order

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} An order, decision or action referred to in
  subsection (2) takes effect as of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the day that immediately follows the last day of
    the thirty day period referred to in that subsection, where no
    hearing is requested under subsection (3); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the day that the order or decision is made or the
    action is taken by the Regulator, where a hearing is requested under
    subsection (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Judicial review

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} Any order, decision or action in respect of which a
  hearing is held under this section is subject to review and to be set
  aside by the Supreme Court of Newfoundland and Labrador.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1987, c. 3, s. 124
- 2014, c. 13, s. 21
- [2024, c. 20, s. 50](#1491204-1529759){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491204-1529759.html"}
- [2024, c. 20, s. 101](#1491355-1530775){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491355-1530775.html"}
- [2024, c. 20, s. 102(E)](#1491484-1531153){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491484-1531153.html"}
:::

#### [Regulations]{.HTitleText3} {#h-153760 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[125]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-125
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 7, the Governor
  in Council may make regulations for carrying out the purposes and
  provisions of this Part and, without restricting the generality of the
  foregoing, may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} not inconsistent with the [Canada Lands Surveys
    Act](/eng/acts/L-6) authorizing or requiring the survey, division
    and subdivision of the offshore area and defining and describing
    those divisions and subdivisions;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the information and documentation to be
    provided by interest owners and interest holders for the purposes of
    this Part, the time when and manner in which such information and
    documentation is to be provided, authorizing the Regulator to
    prescribe the form in which it is to be provided and requiring such
    information and documentation to be provided in accordance with the
    regulations;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} requiring fees and deposits to be paid in respect
    of interests, prescribing the amounts of such fees and deposits, the
    time and manner of their payment and providing for the
    administration of such fees and deposits and the disposition and
    return of deposits; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} prescribing any other matter or thing that by this
    Part is to be prescribed or that is to be done by regulations.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2024, c. 20, s. 51\]]{.Repealed}

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2024, c. 20, s. 51\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1987, c. 3, s. 125
- [2024, c. 20, s. 51](#1491209-1531732){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491209-1531732.html"}
- [2024, c. 20, s. 101](#1491355-1531733){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491355-1531733.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-125-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 125"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Forms

- **[[126]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-126 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Regulator may prescribe any form or any information to be given on a
  form that is by this Part or the regulations to be prescribed and may
  include on any form so prescribed a declaration, to be signed by the
  person completing the form, declaring that the information given by
  that person on the form is, to the best of the knowledge of that
  person, true, accurate and complete.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Forms prescribed or authorized

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every form purporting to be a form prescribed or
  authorized by the Regulator shall be deemed to be a form prescribed by
  the Regulator under this Part unless called in question by the
  Regulator or some person acting for the Regulator or Her Majesty in
  right of Canada or the Province.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Forms not regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where a form or information to be given on a form is
  prescribed by the Regulator pursuant to this Act, it shall be deemed
  not to be a regulation within the meaning of the [Statutory
  Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1987, c. 3, s. 126
- [2024, c. 20, s. 101](#1491355-1530797){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491355-1530797.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-126-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 126"){.PITLink}
:::

### [DIVISION X]{.HLabel2}[Transitional, Consequential and Commencement]{.HTitleText2} {#h-153779 .Subheading}

#### [Transitional]{.HTitleText3} {#h-153780 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exploration agreements extant are
continued

- **[[127]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-127
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where an exploration agreement in
  relation to any portion of the offshore area was entered into or
  negotiations in respect thereof were completed under the Canada Oil
  and Gas Act before the coming into force of this section, that
  exploration agreement shall, for the purposes of this Part, be
  referred to as an exploration licence and shall, subject to this Part,
  have effect in accordance with its terms and conditions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Declarations of significant discovery
  are continued

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a declaration of significant discovery was made
  under section 44 of the Canada Oil and Gas Act and is in force on the
  coming into force of this section, it continues in force as if it were
  made pursuant to section 71 of this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed significant discovery licence

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where, on the coming into force of this section, an
  exploration agreement is continuing in force pursuant to subsection
  16(4) of the Canada Oil and Gas Act, it shall be deemed to be a
  significant discovery licence issued under this Part on the coming
  into force of this section and is subject to this Part.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Replacement of rights

- **[[128]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-128
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 127 and
  subsection 129(2), the petroleum-related interests provided for under
  this Part replace all petroleum rights or prospects of petroleum
  rights acquired or vested in relation to any portion of the offshore
  area prior to the coming into force of this section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No compensation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No party shall have any right to claim or receive any
  compensation, damages, indemnity or other form of relief from Her
  Majesty in right of Canada or from any servant or agent thereof for
  any acquired, vested or future right or entitlement or any prospect
  thereof that is replaced or otherwise affected by this Part, or for
  any duty or liability imposed on that party by this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1987, c. 3, s. 128
- [2024, c. 20, s. 52](#1491210-1529771){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491210-1529771.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-128-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 128"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations continue in force

- **[[129]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-129 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  [Canada Oil and Gas Land
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1518) remain in force to the
  extent that they are consistent with this Part until they are revoked
  or replaced by regulations made under this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Former interests

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} All interests provided by the [Canada Oil and Gas
  Land Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1518) that are in force
  on the coming into force of this section continue in force subject to
  sections 130 to 133.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Petro-Canada rights abrogated

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} All rights of Petro-Canada to acquire further
  interests or shares in interests as a result of the operation of
  section 33, 120 or 121 of the [Canada Oil and Gas Land
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1518) are abrogated as of
  March 5, 1982.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where any portion of the offshore area becomes a
  Crown reserve area on or after April 30, 1980, Petro-Canada shall not
  be entitled to exercise any rights under section 33 of the [Canada Oil
  and Gas Land Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1518) with
  respect to that Crown reserve area.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Dealings not vitiated for failure to
  comply with regulations

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where a person acquires, disposes of or otherwise
  deals in an interest or a share in an interest in respect of which
  Petro-Canada would, but for the circumstances described in
  paragraph (a) or (b), have had any right under section 33, 120 or 121
  of the [Canada Oil and Gas Land
  Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1518), no such acquisition,
  disposition or dealing is vitiated by reason only of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the failure to give Petro-Canada a notice required
    under any of those sections; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the erroneous determination of a Canadian
    participation rate under those regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retrospective application

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (5) has retrospective application to any
  acquisition, disposition or dealing that occurred prior to March 5,
  1982.

- Definition of [[Petro Canada]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[Petro-Canada]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  means the corporation established by the Petro-Canada Act.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Former permits, former special renewal
permits and former exploration agreements

- **[[130]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-130
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to sections 132 and 133, the
  interest owner of a former permit, former special renewal permit or
  former exploration agreement shall, on or before the first anniversary
  date of any such interest following March 5, 1982 or on or before six
  months following such date, whichever is the later, negotiate an
  exploration licence with the Regulator subject to sections 31 to 40.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Surrender

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where an interest owner referred to in subsection (1)
  does not comply with that subsection, the portion of the offshore area
  under the relevant interest is deemed to be surrendered and becomes a
  Crown reserve area.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding anything in this Part, an exploration
  licence under subsection (1) may be extended to include all or any
  portion of the offshore area under the preceding interest and any
  related portions of the offshore area that, immediately prior to such
  extension, were Crown reserve areas.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Where drilling commitment exists

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Where a former special renewal permit or former
  exploration agreement contains provisions for the drilling of one or
  more wells, the Regulator shall offer to issue an exploration licence
  to the interest owner for a term equal to the balance of the term of
  the former special renewal permit or former exploration agreement
  remaining on March 5, 1982 and having the same drilling provisions.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1987, c. 3, s. 130
- [2024, c. 20, s. 101](#1491355-1530800){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491355-1530800.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-130-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 130"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Former leases

- **[[131]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-131
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to sections 132 and 133, the
  interest owner of a former lease shall, on or before the first
  anniversary date of the former lease following March 5, 1982 or on or
  before six months following such date, whichever is the later,
  negotiate an exploration licence with the Regulator subject to
  sections 31 to 40.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Surrender

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where an interest owner referred to in subsection (1)
  does not comply with that subsection, the portion of the offshore area
  under the former lease is deemed to be surrendered and becomes a Crown
  reserve area.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 130(3) applies, with such modifications as
  the circumstances require, to lands that may be included in an
  exploration licence under subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1987, c. 3, s. 131
- [2024, c. 20, s. 101](#1491355-1530810){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491355-1530810.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-131-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 131"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of time

**[[132]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-132 .sectionLabel}** Where an exploration
licence required to be negotiated under section 130 or 131 cannot be
negotiated within the period provided in those sections for any reason
not attributable to the interest owner, the Regulator shall extend that
period to allow for such negotiation within a reasonable time.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1987, c. 3, s. 132
- [2024, c. 20, s. 101](#1491355-1530816){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491355-1530816.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-132-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 132"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consolidated exploration licence

- **[[133]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-133 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} One
  or more interest owners of former permits, former special renewal
  permits, former exploration agreements or former leases may, for the
  purposes of complying with subsection 130(1) or 131(1), negotiate
  together a single exploration licence that would consolidate any
  number or combination of such interests held by those interest owners.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Terms and conditions of exploration
  licence

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to sections 31 to 40, an exploration licence
  negotiated pursuant to subsection (1) shall contain any terms and
  conditions that may be agreed on by the Regulator and the interest
  owners thereof.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1987, c. 3, s. 133
- [2024, c. 20, s. 101](#1491355-1530819){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491355-1530819.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-133-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 133"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Crown share abrogated

**[[134]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-134 .sectionLabel}** For greater certainty,
the reservation to Her Majesty in right of Canada of a Crown share in
any interest granted or entered into under the Canada Oil and Gas Act
prior to the coming into force of this section is abrogated as of the
day this section comes into force.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional

**[[134.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-134.1 .sectionLabel}** Despite subsection
75(3) and subject to subsection 85(1), if the interest holder of an
exploration licence issued before 2017 applies for a declaration of
significant discovery in relation to any portion of the offshore area to
which that licence applies and if the significant discovery is indicated
by the first well on a geological feature that demonstrates, by flow
testing, the existence of hydrocarbons in that feature, any significant
discovery licence issued for that significant discovery area continues
in force, in relation to each portion of the offshore area to which the
licence applies, for as long as the declaration of significant discovery
on the basis of which the licence was issued remains in force in
relation to that portion.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2024, c. 20, s. 53](#1491212-1529776){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491212-1529776.html"}
:::

## [PART III]{.HLabel1}[Petroleum and Offshore Renewable Energy Operations]{.HTitleText1} {#h-153835 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-153836 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[135]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-135 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,

[[Chief Conservation Officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Chief Conservation Officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the person
    designated as the Chief Conservation Officer pursuant to section
    140; ([délégué à l'exploitation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Chief Safety Officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Chief Safety Officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the person
    designated as the Chief Safety Officer pursuant to section
    140; ([délégué à la sécurité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[Committee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[Committee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Oil and Gas Committee
    established by section 141; ([Comité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[lease]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[lease]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an oil and gas lease issued
    pursuant to regulations made in accordance with the [Territorial
    Lands Act](/eng/acts/T-7) and the Public Lands Grants Act and
    includes a production licence issued under Part
    II; ([concession]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[permit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[permit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an exploratory oil and gas
    permit issued pursuant to regulations made in accordance with the
    [Territorial Lands Act](/eng/acts/T-7) and the Public Lands Grants
    Act and includes an exploration agreement entered into under the
    [Canada Oil and Gas Land
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._1518) and any exploration
    agreement or licence that is subject to Part
    II; ([permis]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[pipeline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[pipeline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any pipe or any system or
    arrangement of pipes by which petroleum or water incidental to the
    drilling for or production of petroleum is conveyed from any
    wellhead or other place at which it is produced to any other place,
    or from any place where it is stored, processed or treated to any
    other place, and includes all property of any kind used for the
    purpose of, or in connection with or incidental to, the operation of
    a pipeline in the gathering, transporting, handling and delivery of
    petroleum and, without restricting the generality of the foregoing,
    includes offshore installations or vessels, tanks, surface
    reservoirs, pumps, racks, storage and loading facilities,
    compressors, compressor stations, pressure measuring and controlling
    equipment and fixtures, flow controlling and measuring equipment and
    fixtures, metering equipment and fixtures, and heating, cooling and
    dehydrating equipment and fixtures, but does not include any pipe or
    any system or arrangement of pipes that constitutes a distribution
    system for the distribution of gas to ultimate
    consumers; ([pipe-line]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[well]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[well]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any opening in the ground (not
    being a seismic shot hole) that is made, is to be made or is in the
    process of being made, by drilling, boring or other method,

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for the production of petroleum,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} for the purpose of searching for or obtaining
      petroleum,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} for the purpose of obtaining water to inject into
      an underground formation,

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} for the purpose of injecting gas, air, water or
      other substance into an under ground formation, or

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} for any purpose, if made through sedimentary
      rocks to a depth of at least one hundred and fifty
      metres. ([puits]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- 1987, c. 3, s. 135
- 1992, c. 35, s. 52
:::

### [Purpose]{.HTitleText2} {#h-153853 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Purpose

**[[135.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-135.1 .sectionLabel}** The purpose of this
Part is to promote

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} safety, particularly by encouraging persons exploring
  for and exploiting petroleum or carrying on an offshore renewable
  energy project to maintain a prudent regime for achieving safety;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the protection of the environment;

- [(b.1)]{.lawlabel} accountability in accordance with the "polluter
  pays" principle; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of the exploration for and exploitation of
  petroleum, the conservation of petroleum resources and joint
  production arrangements.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 35, s. 53
- 2015, c. 4, s. 48
- [2024, c. 20, s. 55](#1491216-1529778){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491216-1529778.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-135.1-20160227.html "Link to previous version of section 135.1"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-153863 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

**[[136]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-136 .sectionLabel}** This Part applies in
respect of the exploration and drilling for and the production,
conservation, processing and transportation of petroleum, and in respect
of offshore renewable energy projects, in the offshore area.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1987, c. 3, s. 136
- 1992, c. 35, s. 54(F)
- [2024, c. 20, s. 57](#1491220-1529785){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491220-1529785.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-136-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 136"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Oil and Gas Administration Advisory Council]{.HTitleText2} {#h-153868 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designation

**[[136.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-136.1 .sectionLabel}** The Provincial
Minister may designate one of the members of the Oil and Gas
Administration Advisory Council established by the [Canada Oil and Gas
Operations Act](/eng/acts/O-7).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 35, s. 55
:::

### [Offshore Oil and Gas Training Standards Advisory Board]{.HTitleText2} {#h-153873 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval

**[[136.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-136.2 .sectionLabel}** The Provincial
Minister may approve the establishment of the Offshore Oil and Gas
Training Standards Advisory Board by the federal Ministers pursuant to
subsection 5.5(1) of the [Canada Oil and Gas Operations
Act](/eng/acts/O-7).

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 35, s. 55
:::

### [Prohibition]{.HTitleText2} {#h-153878 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition

**[[137]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-137 .sectionLabel}** No person shall carry
on any work or activity related to the exploration or drilling for or
the production, conservation, processing or transportation of petroleum
in the offshore area unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} that person is the holder of an operating licence
  issued under paragraph 138(1)(a);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} that person is the holder of an authorization issued,
  before the commencement of operations, under paragraph 138(1)(b) for
  each such work or activity; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} where it is required, that person is authorized or
  entitled to carry on business in the place where that person proposes
  to carry on the work or activity.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1987, c. 3, s. 137
- 1992, c. 35, s. 56
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibition --- offshore renewable energy

**[[137.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-137.01 .sectionLabel}** No person shall
carry on any work or activity related to an offshore renewable energy
project unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} that person is the holder of an authorization issued,
  before the commencement of operations, under subsection 138.01(1) for
  the work or activity; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if it is required, that person is authorized or
  entitled to carry on business in the place where that person proposes
  to carry on the work or activity.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2024, c. 20, s. 58](#1491222-1529793){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491222-1529793.html"}
:::

### [Delegation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-153886 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delegation

**[[137.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-137.1 .sectionLabel}** The Regulator may
delegate any of the Regulator's powers under section 138, 138.01, 138.2,
138.3, 139.1, 139.2, 162.1, 163, 183.2 or 183.21 to any person, and the
person shall exercise those powers in accordance with the terms of the
delegation.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 35, s. 57
- 2015, c. 4, s. 49
- [2024, c. 20, s. 59](#1491224-1529794){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491224-1529794.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-137.1-20160227.html "Link to previous version of section 137.1"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Licences and Authorizations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-153891 .Subheading}

#### [Petroleum Operating Licences and Authorizations]{.HTitleText3} {#h-153892 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Licences and authorizations

- **[[138]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Regulator may, on application made in the form and containing the
  information fixed by it, and made in the prescribed manner, issue in
  relation to petroleum-related works or activities

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an operating licence; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to section 45, an authorization with
    respect to each work or activity proposed to be carried on.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Term and renewals

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An operating licence expires on the thirty-first day
  of March immediately after the day on which it is issued and may be
  renewed for successive periods not exceeding one year each.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements for operating licence

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An operating licence is subject to any requirements
  that are determined by the Regulator or that are prescribed and to any
  deposits that are prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copy to Chief Safety Officer

  [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} On receipt by the Regulator of an application for
  an authorization for a work or activity referred to in paragraph
  (1)(b) or of an application to amend such an authorization, the
  Regulator shall provide a copy of the application to the Chief Safety
  Officer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements for authorization

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} An authorization is subject to such approvals as the
  Regulator determines or as may be granted in accordance with the
  regulations and such requirements and deposits as the Regulator
  determines or as may be prescribed, including

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} requirements relating to liability for loss,
    damage, costs or expenses;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} requirements for the carrying out of environmental
    programs or studies;

  - [(b.1)]{.lawlabel} requirements that are conditions established
    under the [Impact Assessment Act](/eng/acts/I-2.75), including those
    established under section 64 of that Act or by regulations made
    under paragraph 112(1)(a.2) of that Act; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} requirements for the payment of expenses incurred
    by the Regulator in approving the design, construction and operation
    of production facilities and production platforms, as those terms
    are defined in the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(4.1)]{.lawlabel} The approvals, requirements and deposits that are
  determined, granted or prescribed shall not be inconsistent with the
  provisions of this Act or the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension or revocation

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Regulator may suspend or revoke an operating
  licence or an authorization for failure to comply with, contravention
  of or default in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a requirement, approval or deposit, determined by
    the Regulator in accordance with the provisions of this Part or Part
    III.1 or granted or prescribed by regulations made under either of
    those Parts, subject to which the licence or authorization was
    issued;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} a fee or charge payable in accordance with
    regulations made under section 29.1;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a requirement undertaken in a declaration referred
    to in subsection 139.1(1);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subsection 139.1(3), 139.2(2), 162.1(4) or (5) or
    163(1.1), (1.2) or (5);

  - [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} any provision of Part III.1; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any applicable regulation.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1987, c. 3, s. 138
- 1992, c. 35, s. 58
- 2014, c. 13, s. 22
- 2015, c. 4, ss. 50, 117
- [2024, c. 20, s. 61](#1491228-1529798){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491228-1529798.html"}
- [2024, c. 20, s. 101](#1491355-1530822){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491355-1530822.html"}
- [2024, c. 20, s. 102(E)](#1491484-1531155){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491484-1531155.html"}
- [2024, c. 20, s. 103(F)](#1491503-1531166){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491503-1531166.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-138-20160227.html "Link to previous version of section 138"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Offshore Renewable Energy Authorizations]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1529805 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorizations

- **[[138.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138.01
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Regulator may, on application
  containing any information required by the Regulator or prescribed,
  issue an authorization with respect to each work or activity proposed
  to be carried out in relation to an offshore renewable energy project.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copy of application

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On receipt by the Regulator of an application for an
  authorization referred to in subsection (1) or of an application to
  amend the authorization, the Regulator shall provide a copy of the
  application to the Chief Safety Officer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Terms and conditions of authorization

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An authorization is subject to any terms and
  conditions required by the Regulator or prescribed, including terms or
  conditions with respect to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} approvals;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} deposits of money;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} liability for loss, damage, costs or expenses
    related to [[debris]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in
    subsection 183.17(1);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the carrying out of safety studies or environmental
    programs or studies;

  - [(d.1)]{.lawlabel} conditions established under the [Impact
    Assessment Act](/eng/acts/I-2.75), including those established under
    section 64 of that Act or by regulations made under paragraph
    112(1)(a.2) of that Act; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} certificates of fitness and who may issue them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The terms and conditions shall not be inconsistent
  with the provisions of this Act or the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension or revocation

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Regulator may suspend or revoke an authorization
  referred to in subsection (1) for failure to comply with,
  contravention of or default in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a term or condition, determined by the Regulator in
    accordance with the provisions of this Part or Part III.1 or
    prescribed under either of those Parts, subject to which the
    authorization was issued;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a fee or charge payable in accordance with
    regulations made under section 29.1;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a requirement undertaken in a declaration referred
    to in subsection 139.1(2);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} subsection 139.1(3), 183.2(3) or 183.21(2);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any provision of Part III.1; or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} any applicable regulation.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 51
- [2024, c. 20, s. 62](#1491233-1529804){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491233-1529804.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-138.01-20160227.html "Link to previous version of section 138.01"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Impact Assessment]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1531542 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[designated
project]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[138.011]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138.011
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section and
  sections 138.012 to 138.016, [[designated
  project]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a [[designated
  project]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in section 2 of the
  [Impact Assessment Act](/eng/acts/I-2.75), that is a work or activity
  referred to in section 137 or 137.01 of this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Impact assessment

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an application for an authorization made under
  paragraph 138(1)(b) or subsection 138.01(1) or an application made
  under subsection 139(2) is in respect of a designated project, the
  Regulator may not make a determination respecting that application
  before

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Impact Assessment Agency of Canada decides,
    under subsection 16(1) of the [Impact Assessment
    Act](/eng/acts/I-2.75), that an impact assessment of that project is
    not required; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Minister of the Environment has issued a
    decision statement under section 65 of that Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designating a work or activity

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the Minister of the Environment considers
  designating, under section 9 of the [Impact Assessment
  Act](/eng/acts/I-2.75), a work or activity referred to in section 137
  or 137.01 of this Act, the Chairperson of the Regulator shall provide
  the Minister with comments respecting this designation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consultation with Ministers

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Before providing the Minister of the Environment with
  comments, the Chairperson of the Regulator may consult with the
  Federal Minister and the Provincial Minister and, in that event, the
  Chairperson shall consult with both Ministers.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2024, c. 20, s. 62.1](#1491238-1531555){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491238-1531555.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access to information by Impact
Assessment Agency

- **[[138.012]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138.012
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Regulator shall provide the
  Impact Assessment Agency of Canada, on request and within the period
  specified by the Agency under subsection 13(1) of the [Impact
  Assessment Act](/eng/acts/I-2.75), with any specialist or expert
  information or knowledge that the Regulator possesses.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Engaging proponent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Regulator shall, on the Agency's request made
  under subsection 13(2) of the [Impact Assessment
  Act](/eng/acts/I-2.75), engage the proponent of the designated project
  in order to specify to the proponent the information, if any, that the
  Regulator may require in order to exercise its powers or perform its
  duties or functions with respect to that project.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Comments for Agency --- notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Chairperson of the Regulator shall provide the
  Agency with comments for any work or activity referred to in section
  137 or 137.01 that is the subject of a notice posted under subsection
  15(3) of the [Impact Assessment Act](/eng/acts/I-2.75) in order to
  assist the Agency to decide whether an impact assessment of that work
  or activity is required.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consultation with Ministers

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Before providing the Agency with comments, the
  Chairperson of the Regulator may consult with the Federal Minister and
  the Provincial Minister and, in that event, the Chairperson shall
  consult with both Ministers.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Comments for Agency --- time limits

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the Agency decides that an impact assessment of
  the designated project is required, the Chairperson of the Regulator
  shall provide the Agency with comments respecting the time limits
  referred to in subsections 28(2) and 37(1) of the [Impact Assessment
  Act](/eng/acts/I-2.75) within which the impact assessment report shall
  be submitted to the Minister of the Environment and within which any
  recommendations shall be posted on the Internet site established by
  the Agency.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Comments --- time extension

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Chairperson of the Regulator shall provide the
  Minister of the Environment with comments respecting any extension to
  the time limits that is considered under subsection 28(6) or (7) or
  37(3) or (4) of the [Impact Assessment Act](/eng/acts/I-2.75).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consultation with Ministers

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Before providing the Minister of the Environment with
  comments, the Chairperson of the Regulator may consult with the
  Federal Minister and the Provincial Minister and, in that event, the
  Chairperson shall consult with both Ministers.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Comments for Agency --- information or
  studies

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} If the Agency decides that an impact assessment of
  the designated project is required, the Regulator shall provide the
  Agency with comments respecting

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the scope of the factors that the Agency takes into
    account in determining, under subsection 18(1.2) of the [Impact
    Assessment Act](/eng/acts/I-2.75), what information or which studies
    it considers necessary and that it may require from the proponent
    for the conduct of the impact assessment;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the information or studies that the Regulator
    considers necessary for the conduct of the impact assessment or
    preparation of the impact assessment report, as the case may be, and
    that the Agency may require from the proponent under paragraph
    18(1)(a), subsections 19(3) and 26(2) and section 38 of that Act;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} whether the proponent has provided the Agency with
    all of the information or studies necessary for the conduct of the
    impact assessment or preparation of the impact assessment report.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2024, c. 20, s. 62.1](#1491238-1531577){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491238-1531577.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access to information by Agency or review
panel

**[[138.013]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138.013 .sectionLabel}** The Regulator
shall provide the Impact Assessment Agency of Canada or a review panel,
on request and within the period specified by the Agency or review panel
under section 23 of the [Impact Assessment Act](/eng/acts/I-2.75), with
any specialist or expert information or knowledge that the Regulator
possesses.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2024, c. 20, s. 62.1](#1491238-1531581){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491238-1531581.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Comments for Agency --- conditions

**[[138.014]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138.014 .sectionLabel}** If the Impact
Assessment Agency of Canada or review panel is to make recommendations
respecting a designated project to assist the Minister of the
Environment in establishing conditions under section 64 of the [Impact
Assessment Act](/eng/acts/I-2.75), including respecting mitigation
measures, a follow-up program or an adaptive management plan, the
Regulator shall provide the Agency or review panel, as the case may be,
with comments respecting those conditions.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2024, c. 20, s. 62.1](#1491238-1531585){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491238-1531585.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Federal authority's obligation

**[[138.015]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138.015 .sectionLabel}** Every [[federal
authority]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in section 2 of the
[Impact Assessment Act](/eng/acts/I-2.75), shall provide the Regulator,
on request and within the period specified by the Regulator, with any
specialist or expert information or knowledge that the authority
possesses and that the Regulator may require in order to

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} decide whether to authorize a work or activity under
  subsection 138(1) or 138.01(1);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} decide whether to approve a development plan under
  subsection 139(4) or any amendment to that plan under subsection
  139(5); or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} conduct a regional assessment under section 138.017
  or a strategic assessment under section 138.018.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2024, c. 20, s. 62.2](#1491240-1531595){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491240-1531595.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access to information by authority

**[[138.016]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138.016 .sectionLabel}** The Regulator
shall provide an [[authority]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in
section 81 of the [Impact Assessment Act](/eng/acts/I-2.75), on request
and within the period specified by the authority under section 85 of
that Act, with any specialist or expert information or knowledge that
the Regulator possesses respecting a [[project]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef},
as defined in section 81 of that Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2024, c. 20, s. 62.2](#1491240-1531599){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491240-1531599.html"}
:::

#### [Regional Assessments and Strategic Assessments]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1531600 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regional assessments

- **[[138.017]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138.017
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Regulator may conduct a regional
  assessment of the effects of any existing or future work or activity
  referred to in sections 137 or 137.01.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agreement between Ministers

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Federal Minister and the Provincial Minister may
  enter into an agreement with any jurisdiction authorized under any
  other federal or provincial legislation to conduct a regional
  assessment of the effects of any existing or future work or activity
  referred to in sections 137 or 137.01, including to specify the time
  limits and terms of that regional assessment.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2024, c. 20, s. 62.2](#1491240-1531607){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491240-1531607.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Strategic assessments

- **[[138.018]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138.018
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Regulator may conduct a
  strategic assessment of any proposed or existing policy, plan or
  program respecting the offshore area or of any issue that is relevant
  to any existing or future work or activity referred to in sections 137
  or 137.01.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agreement between Ministers

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Federal Minister and the Provincial Minister may
  enter into an agreement with any jurisdiction authorized under any
  other federal or provincial legislation to conduct a strategic
  assessment of any proposed or existing policy, plan or program
  respecting the offshore area or of any issue that is relevant to any
  existing or future work or activity referred to in sections 137 or
  137.01, including to specify the time limits and terms of that
  strategic assessment.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2024, c. 20, s. 62.2](#1491240-1531614){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491240-1531614.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access to information by Agency or
committee

**[[138.019]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138.019 .sectionLabel}** The Regulator
shall provide the Impact Assessment Agency of Canada or a committee, on
request and within the period specified by the Agency or committee under
section 100 of the [Impact Assessment Act](/eng/acts/I-2.75), with any
specialist or expert information or knowledge that the Regulator
possesses.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2024, c. 20, s. 62.2](#1491240-1531618){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491240-1531618.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Comments for Minister of the Environment

**[[138.02]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138.02 .sectionLabel}** If the Minister
of the Environment shall, for the purposes of any regional assessment of
the effects of any existing or future work or activity referred to in
sections 137 or 137.01 or for the purposes of any strategic assessment
in the offshore area, establish the terms of reference and appoint
members of a committee or establish the terms of reference of the Impact
Assessment Agency of Canada under section 96 of the [Impact Assessment
Act](/eng/acts/I-2.75), the Federal Minister, the Provincial Minister
and the Chairperson of the Regulator shall provide the Minister of the
Environment with comments respecting those terms of reference or
appointments.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 51
- [2024, c. 20, s. 62.2](#1491240-1531588){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491240-1531588.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-138.02-20160227.html "Link to previous version of section 138.02"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Participant Funding Program]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1531619 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Participant funding program

**[[138.021]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138.021 .sectionLabel}** The Regulator
may establish a participant funding program to facilitate the
participation of the public and any Indigenous peoples of Canada in
consultations concerning any matter respecting the offshore area.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2024, c. 20, s. 62.2](#1491240-1531623){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491240-1531623.html"}
:::

#### [Right of Entry]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1531624 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right of entry

- **[[138.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), any
  person may, for the purpose of exploring for or exploiting petroleum,
  enter on and use any portion of the offshore area in order to carry on
  a work or activity authorized under paragraph 138(1)(b).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right of entry --- offshore renewable
  energy

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), any person may, for the
  purpose of carrying on an offshore renewable energy project, enter on
  and use any portion of the offshore area in order to carry on a work
  or activity authorized under subsection 138.01(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right of entry --- abandoned facilities

  [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), any person may enter on
  and use any portion of the offshore area in order to make contact
  with, alter or remove an abandoned facility if they have been
  authorized to do so under subsection 183.3(2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a person occupies a portion of the offshore area
  under a lawful right or title, other than an authorization under
  paragraph 138(1)(b) or subsection 138.01(1) or an
  [[interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in section 47, no person
  may enter on or use that portion for a purpose referred to in any of
  subsections (1) to (1.2) without the consent of the occupier or, if
  consent has been refused, except in accordance with the terms and
  conditions imposed by a decision of an arbitrator made in accordance
  with the regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 35, s. 58
- [2024, c. 20, s. 63](#1491242-1531625){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491242-1531625.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-138.1-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 138.1"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Safety of Works and Activities]{.HTitleText3} {#h-153950 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Safety --- petroleum operations

- **[[138.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Regulator shall, before issuing
  an authorization for a work or activity referred to in paragraph
  138(1)(b), consider the safety of the work or activity by reviewing,
  in consultation with the Chief Safety Officer, the system as a whole
  and its components, including its structures, facilities, equipment,
  operating procedures and personnel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Safety --- offshore renewable energy
  operations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Regulator shall, before a work or activity
  referred to in subsection 138.01(1) commences, consider the safety of
  the work or activity by reviewing, in consultation with the Chief
  Safety Officer, the system as a whole and its components, including
  its structures, facilities, equipment, operating procedures and
  personnel.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1992, c. 35, s. 58
- [2024, c. 20, s. 64](#1491247-1529832){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491247-1529832.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-138.2-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 138.2"){.PITLink}
:::

#### [Spill-treating Agent]{.HTitleText3} {#h-153955 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Net environmental benefit

**[[138.21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-138.21 .sectionLabel}** The Regulator
shall not permit the use of a spill-treating agent in an authorization
issued under paragraph 138(1)(b) unless the Regulator determines, taking
into account any prescribed factors and any factors the Regulator
considers appropriate, that the use of the spill-treating agent is
likely to achieve a net environmental benefit.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 52
- [2024, c. 20, s. 101](#1491355-1530830){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491355-1530830.html"}
:::
