[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order of court

- **[[196]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-196 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  person is found guilty of an offence under this Part, the court may,
  having regard to the nature of the offence and the circumstances
  surrounding its commission, in addition to any other punishment that
  may be imposed under this Part, make an order that has any or all of
  the following effects:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prohibiting the offender from committing an act or
    engaging in an activity that may, in the opinion of the court,
    result in the continuation or repetition of the offence;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} directing the offender to take any action that the
    court considers appropriate to remedy or avoid any harm to the
    environment that results or may result from the act or omission that
    constituted the offence;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} directing the offender to take any measures that
    the court considers appropriate to avoid any injury or damage that
    may result from the act or omission that constituted the offence, or
    to remedy any injury or damage resulting from it;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} directing the offender to carry out environmental
    effects monitoring in the manner established by the Regulator or
    directing the offender to pay, in the manner specified by the court,
    an amount of money for the purposes of environmental effects
    monitoring;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} directing the offender to make changes to their
    environmental management system that are satisfactory to the
    Regulator;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} directing the offender to have an environmental
    audit conducted by a person of a class and at the times specified by
    the Regulator and directing the offender to remedy any deficiencies
    revealed during the audit;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} directing the offender to pay to Her Majesty in
    right of Canada, for the purpose of promoting the conservation,
    protection or restoration of the environment, or to pay into the
    Environmental Damages Fund --- an account in the accounts of Canada
    --- an amount of money that the court considers appropriate;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} directing the offender to pay to the Regulator an
    amount of money that the court considers appropriate for the purpose
    of conducting research, education and training in matters related to
    the protection of the environment, conservation of petroleum
    resources or safety of petroleum operations or offshore renewable
    energy projects;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} directing the offender to publish, in the manner
    specified by the court, the facts relating to the commission of the
    offence and the details of the punishment imposed, including any
    orders made under this subsection;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} directing the offender to submit to the Chief
    Safety Officer, on application by the Chief Safety Officer within
    three years after the conviction, any information with respect to
    the offender's activities that the court considers appropriate in
    the circumstances;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} directing the offender to notify, at the offender's
    own cost and in the manner specified by the court, any person
    aggrieved or affected by the offender's conduct of the facts
    relating to the commission of the offence and of the details of the
    punishment imposed, including any orders made under this subsection;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} directing the offender to post a bond or pay an
    amount of money into court that the court considers appropriate to
    ensure that the offender complies with any prohibition, direction,
    requirement or condition that is specified in the order;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} directing the offender to perform community
    service, subject to any reasonable conditions that may be imposed by
    the court;

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} directing the offender to pay, in the manner
    specified by the court, an amount of money to environmental, health
    or other groups to assist in their work;

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} directing the offender to pay, in the manner
    specified by the court, an amount of money to an educational
    institution including for scholarships for students enrolled in
    studies related to the environment;

  - [(p)]{.lawlabel} requiring the offender to comply with any
    conditions that the court considers appropriate in the circumstances
    for securing the offender's good conduct and for preventing the
    offender from repeating the same offence or committing another
    offence under this Part;

  - [(q)]{.lawlabel} prohibiting the offender from taking measures to
    acquire an interest or from applying for any new licence or other
    authorization under this Act during any period that the court
    considers appropriate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Coming into force and duration of order

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An order made under subsection (1) comes into force
  on the day on which the order is made or on any other day that the
  court may determine, but shall not continue in force for more than
  three years after that day.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If an offender does not comply with an order
  requiring the publication of facts relating to the offence and the
  details of the punishment, the Regulator may, in the manner that the
  court directed the offender, publish those facts and details and
  recover the costs of publication from the offender.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debt due to Regulator

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the Regulator incurs publication costs under
  subsection (3), the costs constitute a debt due to the Regulator and
  may be recovered in any court of competent jurisdiction.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1987, c. 3, s. 196
- 2015, c. 4, ss. 65, 117
- [2024, c. 20, s. 83](#1491314-1530331){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491314-1530331.html"}
- [2024, c. 20, s. 101](#1491355-1530974){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491355-1530974.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-196-20160227.html "Link to previous version of section 196"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Variation of sanctions

- **[[196.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-196.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), if a
  court has made, in relation to an offender, an order under section
  196, the court may, on application by the offender or the Regulator,
  require the offender to appear before it and, after hearing the
  offender and the Regulator, vary the order in one or more of the
  following ways that the court considers appropriate because of a
  change in the offender's circumstances since the order was made:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} by making changes to any prohibition, direction,
    requirement or condition that is specified in the order for any
    period or by extending the period during which the order is to
    remain in force, not exceeding one year; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} by decreasing the period during which the order is
    to remain in force or by relieving the offender of compliance with
    any condition that is specified in the order, either absolutely or
    partially or for any period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Before making an order under subsection (1), the
  court may direct that notice be given to any persons that the court
  considers to be interested, and may hear any of those persons.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 65
- [2024, c. 20, s. 101](#1491355-1530982){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491355-1530982.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-196.1-20160227.html "Link to previous version of section 196.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsequent applications with leave

**[[196.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-196.2 .sectionLabel}** If an application
made under subsection 196.1(1) in relation to an offender has been heard
by a court, no other application may be made under section 196.1 in
relation to the offender except with leave of the court.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 65
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recovery of fines and amounts

**[[196.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-196.3 .sectionLabel}** If a person is
convicted of an offence under this Part and a fine that is imposed is
not paid when required or if a court orders an offender to pay an amount
under subsection 196(1) or 196.1(1), the prosecutor may, by filing the
conviction or order, as the case may be, enter as a judgment the amount
of the fine or the amount ordered to be paid, and costs, if any, in the
Supreme Court of Newfoundland and Labrador, and the judgment is
enforceable against the person in the same manner as if it were a
judgment rendered against them in that Court in civil proceedings.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 65
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuing offences

**[[197]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-197 .sectionLabel}** Where an offence under
this Part is committed on more than one day or is continued for more
than one day, it shall be deemed to be a separate offence for each day
on which the offence is committed or continued.

**[[198]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-198 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed, 1992, c.
35, s. 83\]]{.Repealed}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time limited for summary conviction
proceedings

**[[199]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-199 .sectionLabel}** Proceedings by way of
summary conviction for an offence under this Part may be instituted at
any time within but no later than three years after the day on which the
subject-matter of the proceedings arose, unless the prosecutor and the
defendant otherwise agree.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1987, c. 3, s. 199
- 2014, c. 13, s. 41
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-199-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 199"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Evidence

**[[200]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-200 .sectionLabel}** In any prosecution for
an offence under this Part, a copy of any order or other document
purporting to have been made pursuant to this Part or the regulations
and purporting to have been signed by the person authorized by this Part
or the regulations to make that order or document is, in the absence of
any evidence to the contrary, proof of the matters set out therein.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1987, c. 3, s. 200
- 2014, c. 13, s. 42(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-200-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 200"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Jurisdiction of judge or justice

**[[201]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-201 .sectionLabel}** Any complaint or
information in respect of an offence under this Part may be heard, tried
or determined by a justice or judge if the accused is resident or
carrying on business within the territorial jurisdiction of that justice
or judge although the matter of the complaint or information did not
arise in that territorial jurisdiction.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Action to enjoin not prejudiced by
prosecution

- **[[202]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding that a prosecution
  has been instituted in respect of an offence under this Part, the
  regulations or any order made pursuant to this Part or the
  regulations, the Regulator may commence and maintain an action to
  enjoin the committing of any contravention of this Part, the
  regulations or any order made pursuant to this Part or the
  regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Civil remedy not affected

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No civil remedy for any act or omission is suspended
  or affected by reason that the act or omission is an offence under
  this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1987, c. 3, s. 202
- [2024, c. 20, s. 101](#1491355-1530986){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491355-1530986.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-202-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 202"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information

**[[202.001]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202.001 .sectionLabel}** In any
proceedings for an offence under this Part

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an information may include more than one offence
  committed by the same person;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} all those offences may be tried concurrently; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} one conviction for any or all offences so included
  may be made.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 13, s. 43
- 2015, c. 4, s. 117
:::

#### [Administrative Monetary Penalties]{.HTitleText3} {#h-154912 .Subheading}

##### [Powers]{.HTitleText4} {#h-154913 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[202.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202.01
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 7, the Governor
  in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} designating as a violation that may be proceeded
    with in accordance with this Part

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the contravention of any specified provision of
      this Part or of any of its regulations,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the contravention of any direction, requirement,
      decision or order, or of any direction, requirement, decision or
      order of a specified class of directions, requirements or orders,
      made under this Part, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the failure to comply with any term or
      condition of

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} an operating licence or authorization, or a
        specified class of operating licences or authorizations, issued
        under this Part, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} any approval or exemption or a specified class
        of approvals or exemptions, granted under this Part;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} respecting the determination of, or the method of
    determining, the amount payable as the penalty, which may be
    different for individuals and other persons, for each violation; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting the service of documents required or
    authorized under section 202.06, 202.2 or 202.5, including the
    manner and proof of service and the circumstances under which
    documents are considered to be served.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The amount that may be determined under any
  regulations made under paragraph (1)(b) as the penalty for a violation
  shall not be more than \$25,000, in the case of an individual, and
  \$100,000, in the case of any other person.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 66
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers

**[[202.02]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202.02 .sectionLabel}** The Regulator may

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} establish the form of notices of violation;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} designate persons or classes of persons who are
  authorized to issue notices of violation;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} establish, in respect of each violation, a short-form
  description to be used in notices of violation; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} designate persons or classes of persons to conduct
  reviews under section 202.4.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 66
- [2024, c. 20, s. 101](#1491355-1530988){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491355-1530988.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-202.02-20160227.html "Link to previous version of section 202.02"){.PITLink}
:::

##### [Violations]{.HTitleText4} {#h-154937 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Commission of violation

- **[[202.03]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202.03
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person who contravenes or
  fails to comply with a provision, direction, requirement, decision or
  order, or term or condition the contravention of which, or the failure
  to comply with which, is designated to be a violation by a regulation
  made under paragraph 202.01(1)(a) commits a violation and is liable to
  a penalty of an amount to be determined in accordance with the
  regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Purpose of penalty

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The purpose of the penalty is to promote compliance
  with this Part and not to punish.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 66
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liability of directors, officers, etc.

**[[202.04]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202.04 .sectionLabel}** If a corporation
commits a violation, any director, officer, or agent or mandatary of the
corporation who directed, authorized, assented to, acquiesced in or
participated in the commission of the violation is a party to the
violation and is liable to a penalty of an amount to be determined in
accordance with the regulations, whether or not the corporation has been
proceeded against in accordance with this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 66
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof of violation

**[[202.05]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202.05 .sectionLabel}** In any
proceedings under this Part against a person in relation to a violation,
it is sufficient proof of the violation to establish that it was
committed by an employee, or agent or mandatary, of the person, whether
or not the employee, agent or mandatary is identified or proceeded
against in accordance with this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 66
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issuance and service of notice of
violation

- **[[202.06]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202.06
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a person designated under
  paragraph 202.02(b) believes on reasonable grounds that a person has
  committed a violation, the designated person may issue a notice of
  violation and cause it to be served on the person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The notice of violation shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} name the person that is believed to have committed
    the violation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} set out the relevant facts surrounding the
    violation;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} set out the amount of the penalty for the
    violation;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} inform the person of their right, under section
    202.2, to request a review with respect to the amount of the penalty
    or the facts of the violation, and of the period within which that
    right is to be exercised;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} inform the person of the manner of paying the
    penalty set out in the notice; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} inform the person that, if they do not pay the
    penalty or exercise their right referred to in paragraph (d), they
    will be considered to have committed the violation and that they are
    liable to the penalty set out in the notice.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 66
:::

##### [Rules about Violations]{.HTitleText4} {#h-154966 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certain defences not available

- **[[202.07]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202.07
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A person named in a notice of
  violation does not have a defence by reason that the person

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} exercised due diligence to prevent the commission
    of the violation; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} reasonably and honestly believed in the existence
    of facts that, if true, would exonerate the person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Common law principles

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every rule and principle of the common law that
  renders any circumstance a justification or excuse in relation to a
  charge for an offence under this Part applies in respect of a
  violation to the extent that it is not inconsistent with this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 66
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuing violation

**[[202.08]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202.08 .sectionLabel}** A violation that
is committed or continued on more than one day constitutes a separate
violation for each day on which it is committed or continued.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 66
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Violation or offence

- **[[202.09]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202.09
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Proceeding with any act or omission
  as a violation under this Part precludes proceeding with it as an
  offence under this Part, and proceeding with it as an offence under
  this Part precludes proceeding with it as a violation under this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Violations not offences

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, a violation is not an offence
  and, accordingly, section 126 of the [Criminal Code](/eng/acts/C-46)
  does not apply in respect of a violation.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 66
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation or prescription period

**[[202.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202.1 .sectionLabel}** No notice of
violation is to be issued more than two years after the day on which the
matter giving rise to the violation occurred.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 66
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-202.1-20141231.html "Link to previous version of section 202.1"){.PITLink}
:::

##### [Reviews]{.HTitleText4} {#h-154991 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to request review

**[[202.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202.2 .sectionLabel}** A person who is
served with a notice of violation may, within 30 days after the day on
which it is served, or within any longer period that the Regulator
allows, make a request to the Regulator for a review of the amount of
the penalty or the facts of the violation, or both.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 66
- [2024, c. 20, s. 101](#1491355-1530990){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491355-1530990.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-202.2-20160227.html "Link to previous version of section 202.2"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Correction or cancellation of notice of
violation

**[[202.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202.3 .sectionLabel}** At any time before
a request for a review in respect of a notice of violation is received
by the Regulator, a person designated under paragraph 202.02(b) may
cancel the notice of violation or correct an error in it.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 66
- [2024, c. 20, s. 101](#1491355-1530993){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491355-1530993.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-202.3-20160227.html "Link to previous version of section 202.3"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Review

- **[[202.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On receipt of a request made under
  section 202.2, the Regulator shall conduct the review or cause the
  review to be conducted by a person designated under paragraph
  202.02(d).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Regulator shall conduct the review if the notice
  of violation was issued by a person designated under paragraph
  202.02(d).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 66
- [2024, c. 20, s. 101](#1491355-1530996){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491355-1530996.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-202.4-20160227.html "Link to previous version of section 202.4"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Object of review

- **[[202.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202.5
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Regulator or the person
  conducting the review shall determine, as the case may be, whether the
  amount of the penalty for the violation was determined in accordance
  with the regulations or whether the person committed the violation, or
  both.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Regulator or the person conducting the review
  shall render a determination and the reasons for it in writing and
  cause the person who requested the review to be served with a copy of
  them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Correction of penalty

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the Regulator or the person conducting the review
  determines that the amount of the penalty for the violation was not
  determined in accordance with the regulations, the Regulator or the
  person, as the case may be, shall correct the amount of the penalty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Responsibility

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the Regulator or the person conducting the review
  determines that the person who requested the review committed the
  violation, the person who requested the review is liable to the
  penalty as set out in the notice issued under section 202.06 or as set
  out in the determination if the amount of the penalty was corrected
  under subsection (3).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Determination final

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A determination made under this section is final and
  binding and, subject to review by the Supreme Court of Newfoundland
  and Labrador, is not subject to appeal or to review by any court.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 66
- [2024, c. 20, s. 84](#1491316-1530337){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491316-1530337.html"}
- [2024, c. 20, s. 101](#1491355-1531002){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491355-1531002.html"}
- [2024, c. 20, s. 102(E)](#1491484-1531160){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491484-1531160.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-202.5-20160227.html "Link to previous version of section 202.5"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Burden of proof

**[[202.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202.6 .sectionLabel}** If the facts of a
violation are reviewed, the person who issued the notice of violation
shall establish, on a balance of probabilities, that the person named in
it committed the violation identified in it.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 66
:::

##### [Responsibility]{.HTitleText4} {#h-155024 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment

**[[202.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202.7 .sectionLabel}** If a person pays
the penalty set out in a notice of violation, the person is considered
to have committed the violation and proceedings in respect of it are
ended.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 66
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Failure to act

**[[202.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202.8 .sectionLabel}** A person that
neither pays the penalty imposed under this Part nor requests a review
within the period referred to in section 202.2 is considered to have
committed the violation and is liable to the penalty.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 66
:::

##### [Recovery of Penalties]{.HTitleText4} {#h-155033 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Debt to His Majesty

- **[[202.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202.9
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A penalty constitutes a debt due to
  His Majesty in right of the Province and may be recovered in the
  Supreme Court of Newfoundland and Labrador.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No proceedings to recover the debt are to be
  instituted more than five years after the day on which the debt
  becomes payable.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 66
- [2024, c. 20, s. 85](#1491318-1530341){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491318-1530341.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-202.9-20160227.html "Link to previous version of section 202.9"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate

- **[[202.91]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202.91
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Regulator may issue a
  certificate of non-payment certifying the unpaid amount of any debt
  referred to in subsection 202.9(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Registration in the Supreme Court of Newfoundland and
  Labrador of a certificate of non-payment issued under subsection (1)
  has the same effect as a judgment of that court for a debt of the
  amount specified in the certificate and all related registration
  costs.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 66
- [2024, c. 20, s. 86](#1491320-1530343){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491320-1530343.html"}
- [2024, c. 20, s. 101](#1491355-1531008){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491355-1531008.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-202.91-20160227.html "Link to previous version of section 202.91"){.PITLink}
:::

##### [General]{.HTitleText4} {#h-155048 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Admissibility of documents

**[[202.92]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202.92 .sectionLabel}** In the absence of
evidence to the contrary, a document that appears to be a notice issued
under subsection 202.06(1) is presumed to be authentic and is proof of
its contents in any proceeding in respect of a violation.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 66
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication

**[[202.93]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202.93 .sectionLabel}** The Regulator may
make public the nature of a violation, the name of the person who
committed it and the amount of the penalty.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2015, c. 4, s. 66
- [2024, c. 20, s. 101](#1491355-1531010){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491355-1531010.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-202.93-20160227.html "Link to previous version of section 202.93"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Regulations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-155057 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

**[[203]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-203 .sectionLabel}** Subject to section 7,
the Governor in Council may make such regulations not inconsistent with
this Part as may be deemed necessary for carrying out the purposes of
this Part, and, without limiting the generality of the foregoing, may
make regulations defining and distinguishing more particularly for the
purposes of Divisions I and II the expressions "oil" and "gas".

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-155060 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

**[[204]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-204 .sectionLabel}** This Part applies to
every interest or right in petroleum acquired or vested before the
coming into force of this section and is binding on Her Majesty in right
of Canada or a province.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1987, c. 3, s. 204
- 2014, c. 13, s. 44(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-204-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 204"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Transitional]{.HTitleText2} {#h-155065 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Operating licences

- **[[205]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-205
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where an operating licence was
  issued under subsection 3.2(1) of the Oil and Gas Production and
  Conservation Act and is in force on the coming into force of this
  section, it shall be deemed to be an operating licence issued by the
  Regulator under this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorizations and development plan
  approval

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where, prior to the coming into force of this
  section, authorization for any work or activity or approval of a
  development plan was given under subsection 3.2(1) of the Oil and Gas
  Production and Conservation Act or any regulation made under that Act,
  the authorization or approval shall be deemed to have been given by
  the Regulator under this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1987, c. 3, s. 205
- [2024, c. 20, s. 101](#1491355-1531020){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491355-1531020.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-205-20021231.html "Link to previous version of section 205"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART III.1]{.HLabel1}[Occupational Health and Safety]{.HTitleText1} {#h-155071 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-155072 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[205.001]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-205.001
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in
  this Part.

  [[authorization]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[authorization]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an authorization
      issued under paragraph 138(1)(b) or subsection
      138.01(1). ([autorisation]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Chief Safety Officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Chief Safety Officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the person
      designated as the Chief Safety Officer under section
      140. ([délégué à la sécurité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[committee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[committee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a special committee and a
      workplace committee. ([comité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[coordinator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[coordinator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an employee designated
      under subsection 205.045(1) to act as an occupational health and
      safety coordinator. ([coordonnateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[declaration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[declaration]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a declaration referred
      to in subsection 139.1(1) or (2). ([déclaration]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[employee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[employee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual who, in
      return for monetary compensation, performs work or services for an
      employer in respect of a work or activity for which an
      authorization has been issued. ([employé]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[employer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[employer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who employs or
      contracts for the services of any individual in respect of a work
      or activity for which an authorization has been issued, if that
      person has the power to exercise direction and control over the
      individual's work at the workplace. ([employeur]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[hazardous substance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[hazardous substance]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a controlled
      product and any chemical, biological or physical agent that, by
      reason of a property that the agent possesses, is hazardous to the
      health or safety of an individual exposed to it. ([substance
      dangereuse]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[health and safety officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[health and safety officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      occupational health and safety officer or a special
      officer. ([agent de santé et de sécurité]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[interest holder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[interest holder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      section 47. ([Version anglaise
      seulement]{.DefinitionOtherLangOnly})

  [[marine installation or structure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[marine installation or structure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} includes

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any ship, including any ship used for
          construction, production or diving or for geotechnical or
          seismic work,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any offshore drilling unit, including a
          mobile offshore drilling unit,

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} any production platform, subsea
          installation, [[pipeline]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in
          section 135, pumping station or storage structure,

        - [(iii.1)]{.lawlabel} any facility or structure used for
          producing, storing or transmitting an offshore renewable
          energy product, including an electrical substation,

        - [(iii.2)]{.lawlabel} any living accommodation or loading or
          landing platform, and

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} any other work, or work within a class of
          works, prescribed under paragraph (4)(a); but

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} does not include

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} any vessel, including any supply vessel,
          standby vessel, shuttle tanker or seismic chase vessel, that
          provides any supply or support services to a ship,
          installation, structure, work or anything else described in
          paragraph (a), unless the vessel is within a class of vessels
          that is prescribed under paragraph (4)(b), or

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any ship or vessel within a class of ships
          or vessels prescribed under paragraph (4)(c). ([ouvrage en
          mer]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Newfoundland and Labrador social legislation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Newfoundland and Labrador social
      legislation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the provisions of the
      following Acts, as those Acts are amended from time to time: the
      Communicable Diseases Act, R.S.N.L. 1990, c. C-26, the Food and
      Drug Act, R.S.N.L. 1990, c. F-21, the Health and Community
      Services Act, S.N.L. 1995, c. P-37.1, the Human Rights Act, 2010,
      S.N.L. 2010, c. H-13.1, the Labour Standards Act, R.S.N.L.
      1990, c. L-2, the Public Safety Act, S.N.L. 1996, c. P-41.01, the
      Radiation Health and Safety Act, R.S.N.L. 1990, c. R-1, and the
      Workplace Health, Safety and Compensation Act, R.S.N.L. 1990, c.
      W-11. ([lois sociales]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[occupational health and safety officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[occupational health and safety
      officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual designated by
      the Federal Minister under section 205.071. ([agent de santé et de
      sécurité au travail]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[operator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[operator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who holds an
      authorization. ([exploitant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[owner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[owner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who has a right,
      title or interest, including a leasehold interest, recognized by
      law, in a marine installation or structure that is used or is to
      be used as a workplace, or any entity in which the person has
      vested all or any part of their right, title or
      interest. ([propriétaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[passenger craft]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[passenger craft]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any aircraft or
      vessel used to transport employees to or from a workplace while
      --- and immediately before --- it is transporting them. ([véhicule
      de transport]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes individuals, corporations
      and partnerships. ([personne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[personal protective equipment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[personal protective equipment]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes
      personal protective clothing, personal protective devices and
      personal protective materials. ([équipement de protection
      personnelle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[provider of services]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[provider of services]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who,
      for commercial gain,

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} provides services related to the placement with
        an operator or employer of individuals who, in return for
        monetary compensation, perform work or services for the operator
        or employer at a workplace; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} provides services that affect or could affect
        the health or safety of employees or other individuals at a
        workplace or on a passenger craft, including engineering
        services, architectural services, the services of a certifying
        authority referred to in subsection 139.2(6), or the services of
        any person who provides information or advice, issues a
        certificate or affixes a professional seal or
        stamp. ([fournisseur de services]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[provincial labour relations board]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[provincial labour relations board]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      the Labour Relations Board continued under the Labour Relations
      Act, R.S.N.L. 1990, c. L-1, as amended from time to
      time. ([Version anglaise seulement]{.DefinitionOtherLangOnly})

  [[Provincial Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Provincial Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the minister of
      the government of the Province who is responsible for occupational
      health and safety. ([ministre provincial]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[special committee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[special committee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a special
      committee established under section 205.046. ([comité
      spécial]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[special officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[special officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual
      designated under section 205.072. ([agent
      spécial]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[supervisor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[supervisor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an employee who is in
      charge of a workplace or part of a workplace or who has authority
      over other employees. ([superviseur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[supplier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[supplier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who, for
      commercial gain, manufactures, supplies, sells, leases,
      distributes or installs any tool, equipment, machine or device,
      any biological, chemical, or physical agent or any other
      prescribed thing, to be used at a workplace or on a passenger
      craft. ([fournisseur de biens]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[union]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[union]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a trade union as defined in
      the Labour Relations Act, R.S.N.L. 1990, c. L-1, as amended from
      time to time, that has the status of a bargaining agent under that
      Act in respect of any bargaining unit at a workplace, or any
      organization representing employees that has exclusive bargaining
      rights under any other Act of the Legislature of the Province in
      respect of those employees. ([syndicat]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[workplace]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[workplace]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any marine installation or structure where an
        employee is employed in connection with a work or activity for
        which an authorization has been issued;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any workboat used by an employee, and operated
        from a marine installation or structure, to perform routine
        maintenance or repair work in connection with a work or activity
        for which an authorization has been issued; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any dive site from which, and any underwater
        area at which, a diving operation is conducted by an employee in
        connection with a work or activity for which an authorization
        has been issued. ([lieu de travail]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[workplace committee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[workplace committee]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a workplace
      committee established under section 205.043. ([comité du lieu de
      travail]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Hazardous Products Act](/eng/acts/H-3)
  definitions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In this Part, [[controlled
  product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[hazard symbol]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm},
  [[Ingredient Disclosure List]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm},
  [[label]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} and [[material safety data
  sheet]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} have the same meanings as in section 2 and
  subsection 11(1) of the [Hazardous Products Act](/eng/acts/H-3).
  Subsection 11(2) of that Act also applies for the purposes of this
  Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 7 and on the recommendation of the
  Federal Minister and the Minister of Labour, the Governor in Council
  may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} defining [[danger]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[dive
    site]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, [[diving operation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
    and [[incident]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} for the purposes of this Part;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} amending the definition [[Newfoundland and Labrador
    social legislation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection (1) to add
    any Act of the Legislature of the Province or to remove any Act from
    the definition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 7 and on the recommendation of the
  Federal Minister, the Minister of Labour and the Minister of
  Transport, the Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prescribing a work or a class of works for the
    purposes of subparagraph (a)(iv) of the definition [[marine
    installation or structure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection (1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prescribing a class of vessels for the purposes of
    subparagraph (b)(i) of the definition [[marine installation or
    structure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection (1); and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} prescribing a class of ships or vessels for the
    purposes of subparagraph (b)(ii) of the definition [[marine
    installation or structure]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Employees in transit

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of sections 205.005, 205.007 and
  205.008, an employee is deemed to be at a workplace within the
  offshore area while --- and immediately before --- the employee is
  being transported on a passenger craft between the last point of
  embarkation on shore and the workplace, between the workplace and the
  first point of disembarkation on shore, or between workplaces.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 13, s. 45
- [2024, c. 20, s. 87](#1491322-1530346){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491322-1530346.html"}
:::

### [Her Majesty]{.HTitleText2} {#h-155132 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Binding on Her Majesty

**[[205.002]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-205.002 .sectionLabel}** This Part is
binding on Her Majesty in right of Canada or a province.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 13, s. 45
:::

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-155137 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of Part

- **[[205.003]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-205.003
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This Part applies to and in respect
  of a workplace that is situated within the offshore area for the
  purposes of the exploration or drilling for --- or the production,
  conservation or processing of --- petroleum, or for the purposes of
  offshore renewable energy projects, within the offshore area.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Employees and other passengers in
  transit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} This Part also applies to employees and other
  passengers while --- and immediately before --- being transported on a
  passenger craft between the last point of embarkation on shore and the
  workplace, between the workplace and the first point of disembarkation
  on shore, or between workplaces.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 13, s. 45
- [2024, c. 20, s. 88](#1491324-1530360){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491324-1530360.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-205.003-20141203.html "Link to previous version of section 205.003"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of Parts II and III of
[Canada Labour Code](/eng/acts/L-2)

**[[205.004]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-205.004 .sectionLabel}** Despite
subsections 123(1) and 168(1) of the [Canada Labour Code](/eng/acts/L-2)
and any other Act of Parliament, Parts II and III of the [Canada Labour
Code](/eng/acts/L-2) do not apply to and in respect of a workplace that
is situated within the offshore area for the purposes of the exploration
or drilling for --- or the production, conservation or processing of ---
petroleum, or for the purposes of offshore renewable energy projects,
within the offshore area.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 13, s. 45
- [2024, c. 20, s. 89](#1491326-1530362){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491326-1530362.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-205.004-20141203.html "Link to previous version of section 205.004"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of [Canadian Human Rights
Act](/eng/acts/H-6)

**[[205.005]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-205.005 .sectionLabel}** The [Canadian
Human Rights Act](/eng/acts/H-6) does not apply to or in respect of a
workplace that is situated within the offshore area for the purposes of
the exploration or drilling for --- or the production, conservation or
processing of --- petroleum, or for the purposes of offshore renewable
energy projects, within the offshore area.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 13, s. 45
- [2024, c. 20, s. 89](#1491326-1530365){.wb-lbx

:::

:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of [Non-smokers' Health
Act](/eng/acts/N-23.6)

**[[205.006]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-205.006 .sectionLabel}** The
[Non-smokers' Health Act](/eng/acts/N-23.6) does not apply to or in
respect of a workplace that is situated within the offshore area for the
purposes of the exploration or drilling for --- or the production,
conservation or processing of --- petroleum, or for the purposes of
offshore renewable energy projects, within the offshore area.

:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of Newfoundland and Labrador
social legislation

- **[[205.007]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-205.007
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Newfoundland and Labrador social
  legislation and any regulations made under it apply to and in respect
  of a workplace that is situated within the offshore area for the
  purposes of the exploration or drilling for --- or the production,
  conservation or processing of --- petroleum, or for the purposes of
  offshore renewable energy projects, within the offshore area.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inconsistency or conflict

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In the event of an inconsistency or conflict between
  the provisions of this Act, or any regulations made under it, and the
  provisions of Newfoundland and Labrador social legislation or any
  regulations made under that legislation, the provisions of this Act
  and the regulations made under it prevail to the extent of the
  inconsistency or conflict.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 13, s. 45
- [2024, c. 20, s. 90](#1491328-1530371){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491328-1530371.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-205.007-20141203.html "Link to previous version of section 205.007"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of Labour Relations Act,
R.S.N.L. 1990, c. L-1

- **[[205.008]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-205.008
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Despite section 4 of the [Canada
  Labour Code](/eng/acts/L-2) and any other Act of Parliament, the
  provisions of the Labour Relations Act, R.S.N.L. 1990, c. L-1, as
  amended from time to time, and any regulations made under it, apply to
  and in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a marine installation or structure that is situated
    within the offshore area in connection with the exploration or
    drilling for --- or the production, conservation or processing of
    --- petroleum, or in connection with offshore renewable energy
    projects, within the offshore area and that is in the offshore area
    for the purpose of becoming, or that is, permanently attached to,
    permanently anchored to or permanently resting on the seabed or
    subsoil of the submarine areas of the offshore area;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any workboat used by an employee, and operated from
    a marine installation or structure, to perform routine maintenance
    or repair work in connection with a work or activity for which an
    authorization has been issued; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a dive site from which, and any underwater area at
    which, a diving operation is conducted by an employee in connection
    with a work or activity for which an authorization has been issued.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of Part I of [Canada Labour
  Code](/eng/acts/L-2)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Part I of the [Canada Labour Code](/eng/acts/L-2)
  applies to and in respect of a marine installation or structure that
  is situated within the offshore area in connection with the
  exploration or drilling for --- or the production, conservation or
  processing of --- petroleum, or in connection with offshore renewable
  energy projects, within the offshore area if subsection (1) does not
  apply to or in respect of the marine installation or structure.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 13, s. 45
- [2024, c. 20, s. 91](#1491330-1530373){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491330-1530373.html"}
:::

:::

### [Purpose]{.HTitleText2} {#h-155175 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prevention of accidents and injury

- **[[205.009]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-205.009
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The purpose of this Part is to
  prevent accidents and injury arising out of, linked to or occurring in
  the course of employment to which this Part applies, in particular by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} allocating responsibility for occupational health
    and safety among the Regulator and the persons, unions and
    committees having obligations under this Part; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} establishing a framework for them to exercise their
    rights and carry out their obligations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Preventive measures

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Preventive measures should first aim at the
  elimination of hazards, then the reduction of the risks posed by the
  hazards and finally, the taking of protective measures, all with the
  goal of ensuring the health and safety of employees.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 13, s. 45
- [2024, c. 20, s. 101](#1491355-1531021){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491355-1531021.html"}
- [2024, c. 20, s. 103(F)](#1491503-1531167){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491503-1531167.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-205.009-20141203.html "Link to previous version of section 205.009"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Allocation of Responsibility]{.HTitleText2} {#h-155185 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Principles

- **[[205.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-205.01
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The allocation of responsibility for
  occupational health and safety is based on the following principles:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} operators have overall responsibility; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} operators, employers, suppliers, providers of
    services, employees, supervisors, owners and interest holders have
    individual and shared responsibilities, and are responsible for
    cooperating with each other and coordinating their activities
    related to occupational health and safety.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Specific obligations not to limit
  general obligations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the imposition of any specific
  obligation under this Part shall not be construed as limiting the
  generality of any other obligation under this Part.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 13, s. 45
:::

### [Duties of Operators]{.HTitleText2} {#h-155195 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty to establish occupational health and
safety policy

- **[[205.011]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-205.011
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every operator shall develop an
  occupational health and safety policy governing its workplaces.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The policy shall be set out in writing and contain

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the commitments of the operator related to
    occupational health and safety, including its commitment to
    cooperate with employees with regard to health and safety;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the responsibilities of the employers at any of the
    operator's workplaces related to occupational health and safety; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any prescribed requirements.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty to review

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The operator shall review the policy at least every
  three years in consultation with each workplace committee that it
  establishes and with each employer at any of the operator's
  workplaces.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 13, s. 45
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty to take reasonable measures

**[[205.012]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-205.012 .sectionLabel}** Every operator
shall take all reasonable measures to ensure the health and safety of
all employees and other individuals at its workplaces and of all
employees or other passengers while --- and immediately before --- being
transported on a passenger craft to or from any of those workplaces.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 13, s. 45
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Specific duties --- workplace

- **[[205.013]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-205.013
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every operator shall, in respect of
  each of its workplaces,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} ensure the coordination of all work and activities
    for which an authorization has been issued to the operator;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} comply with its occupational health and safety
    management system, and ensure that all employers, supervisors and
    employees at, owners of and providers of services to the workplace
    comply with that system;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} ensure that information necessary for the health
    and safety of employees and other individuals at the workplace is
    communicated to them;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} ensure that all employers, supervisors and
    employees at, owners of and suppliers and providers of services to
    the workplace comply with the provisions of this Part and the
    regulations made under this Part;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} ensure that each employee at the workplace is made
    aware of known or foreseeable health or safety hazards;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} ensure that all work and activities for which an
    authorization has been issued are conducted so as to minimize the
    exposure to hazards, including hazardous substances, of all
    employees and other individuals at the workplace;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} ensure that any installations, facilities,
    equipment and materials at the workplace are properly installed,
    stored and maintained and are safe for their intended use;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} ensure that all employees and other individuals at
    the workplace conduct themselves so as to minimize their exposure to
    hazards, including hazardous substances;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} ensure that all employees and other individuals at
    the workplace are provided with the facilities and personal
    protective equipment --- including any that are prescribed ---
    necessary for their health and safety;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} ensure that all employees and other individuals at
    the workplace are provided with the information and training ---
    including any that are prescribed --- required for the proper use of
    personal protective equipment that is prescribed or that is required
    by the operator to be used or worn;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} ensure that all employees and other individuals at
    the workplace are provided with the instruction, training and
    supervision ---  including any that are prescribed --- necessary for
    their health and safety;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} comply with the occupational health and safety
    requirements of any authorization issued to them, and those
    undertaken in the declaration related to the authorization, and
    record all instances of failures to comply with those requirements
    as well as any measures taken to rectify the failure or to prevent
    further such failures;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} ensure that all employers, supervisors and
    employees at, owners of and providers of services to the workplace
    comply with the occupational health and safety requirements of any
    authorization related to that workplace that is issued to the
    operator, and those undertaken in the declaration related to the
    authorization, and report any instances of failures to comply with
    those requirements to the operator;

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} inform the relevant interest holders of the
    occupational health and safety requirements of any authorization
    related to that workplace that is issued to the operator, and those
    undertaken in the declaration related to the authorization, and of
    any failure to comply with those requirements;

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} ensure that members of committees established for
    the workplace are provided with the support, opportunities and
    training ---  including any that are prescribed --- necessary to
    enable the members to fulfil their duties and functions as a member
    of the committee;

  - [(p)]{.lawlabel} cooperate with those committees and facilitate
    communications between the employees and the committees;

  - [(q)]{.lawlabel} ensure that all or part of the workplace as
    described in paragraphs (a) and (b) of the definition
    [[workplace]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection 205.001(1) is
    inspected by or on behalf of the operator at least once a month, so
    that every part of that workplace is inspected at least once a year,
    and ensure that the workplace committee participates;

  - [(r)]{.lawlabel} ensure that a record is kept of each inspection
    referred to in paragraph (q), including any corrective action taken
    as a consequence; and

  - [(s)]{.lawlabel} cooperate with the Regulator and with persons
    carrying out duties or functions under this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception to paragraph (1)(q)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(q), all or part of a workplace
  described in paragraph (a) of the definition
  [[workplace]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 205.001(1) does not
  have to be inspected at least once a month if that workplace, or that
  part of the workplace, is normally unattended and is used for the
  purpose of carrying out an offshore renewable energy project.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 13, s. 45
- [2024, c. 20, s. 92](#1491335-1530377){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491335-1530377.html"}
- [2024, c. 20, s. 101](#1491355-1531023){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491355-1531023.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-205.013-20141203.html "Link to previous version of section 205.013"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Specific duties --- passenger craft

- **[[205.014]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-205.014
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every operator shall, each time
  before employees or other passengers are transported on a passenger
  craft to or from any of its workplaces,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} ensure that the employees and other passengers are
    provided with any information and instruction --- including any that
    are prescribed --- necessary for their health and safety; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} ensure that the employees are provided with the
    operator's contact information for the purposes of subsection
    205.054(2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Specific duty --- passenger craft

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every operator shall ensure that a passenger craft
  going to or from any of its workplaces

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} meets the requirements of any Act or other law that
    relates to the health or safety of the employees and other
    passengers on the passenger craft; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is equipped with any equipment, devices and
    materials necessary to ensure the health and safety of the employees
    and other passengers, including any that are prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Specific duties --- personal protective
  equipment

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every operator shall ensure that all employees and
  other passengers on a passenger craft going to or from any of its
  workplaces

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} are provided with any personal protective equipment
    necessary to ensure their health and safety, including any that is
    prescribed; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} are provided with the information and training ---
    including any that are prescribed ---  required for the proper use
    of personal protective equipment provided under paragraph (a) and
    the equipment, devices and materials referred to in paragraph
    (2)(b).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 13, s. 45
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Occupational health and safety management
system

- **[[205.015]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-205.015
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every operator shall develop,
  implement and maintain an occupational health and safety management
  system that fosters a culture of workplace safety and that is adapted
  to the circumstances of the work or activity specified in each
  authorization issued to the operator, for the purposes of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} implementing its occupational health and safety
    policy;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} ensuring that the provisions of this Part and the
    regulations made under this Part are complied with; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} complying with the occupational health and safety
    requirements of each of those authorizations, and those undertaken
    in a declaration related to any of those authorizations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The system shall be set out in writing and include
  provisions regarding

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the management of risks to the health and safety of
    employees --- including any prescribed risks --- and procedures for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the ongoing and systematic identification and
      reporting of all hazards,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the assessment of risks associated with
      identified hazards, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the implementation of hazard control measures;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the role of any committee established for any of
    the operator's workplaces and the interaction between those
    committees;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the roles and accountability of the employers,
    employees, providers of services and suppliers that are responsible
    for implementing the operator's occupational health and safety
    policy and occupational health and safety management system;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the allocation of sufficient resources to ensure
    that employees continue to be qualified and competent, that there is
    proper quality control of documents, facilities, equipment and
    materials and that there is effective cooperation among employers;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the procedures for carrying out work or activities,
    dealing with changes in operations and responding to emergencies;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the procedures for dealing with failures to comply
    with the system and the procedures for the reporting and
    investigating of occupational diseases and of accidents, incidents
    and other hazardous occurrences and the keeping of related records
    and statistical analysis;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the auditing of the adequacy and effectiveness of
    the system, including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} determining the ability of the system to achieve
      the purposes set out in subsection (1), and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} identifying improvements that could be made to
      the system; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the implementation of the improvements identified
    during the audit referred to in paragraph (g).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty to review

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The operator shall review the system at least every
  three years in consultation with each workplace committee that it
  establishes.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the regulations establish requirements in respect
  of anything described in any of paragraphs (2)(a) to (h), the system
  shall meet the requirements of those regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 13, s. 45
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power to require code of practice

- **[[205.016]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-205.016
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Chief Safety Officer may, in
  writing, require an operator to establish a code of practice in
  respect of occupational health and safety, or to adopt a code of
  practice in respect of occupational health and safety that is
  specified by the Chief Safety Officer, in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any of its workplaces or any work or activity
    carried out at any of its workplaces; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the transportation of employees to or from any of
    its workplaces.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revision of code of practice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The code of practice may be revised by the Chief
  Safety Officer from time to time, or the Chief Safety Officer may
  require the operator to revise it from time to time.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 13, s. 45
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notification of accidents, etc.

- **[[205.017]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-205.017
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every operator shall, as soon as it
  becomes known to the operator, notify the Chief Safety Officer of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any occupational disease at any of its workplaces;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any accident, incident or other hazardous
    occurrence at any of its workplaces, or on a passenger craft going
    to or from any of those workplaces, that causes a death or serious
    injury or in which a death or serious injury is narrowly avoided.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Investigation of accidents, etc.

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every operator shall investigate any occupational
  disease, or any accident, incident or other hazardous occurrence,
  described in paragraph (1)(a) or (b) and shall keep adequate records
  of its investigation --- including any records that are prescribed ---
  for the period that is prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An operator shall, no later than April 1 of each
  year, submit to each workplace committee that it establishes, to the
  Chief Safety Officer and, on request, to any special committee
  established for any of its workplaces, a written report for the
  immediately preceding calendar year, in a form determined by that
  Officer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of report

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The report shall set out data on all occupational
  diseases, and all accidents, incidents and other hazardous
  occurrences, that have occurred at any of the operator's workplaces or
  on a passenger craft going to or from any of those workplaces during
  the calendar year covered by the report, including the number of
  deaths, the number of serious injuries and the number of minor
  injuries.

- Definition of [[serious injury]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[serious
  injury]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an injury that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} results in the loss by an individual of a body
    member or part of a body member or in the complete loss by an
    individual of the usefulness of a body member or part of a body
    member;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} results in the permanent impairment of a body
    function of an individual; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} prevents an employee from reporting for work or
    from effectively performing all the functions connected with their
    regular work on any day subsequent to the day on which the injury
    occurred, whether or not that subsequent day is a working day for
    them.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 13, s. 45
:::

### [Duties of Employers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-155304 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty to take reasonable measures

**[[205.018]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-205.018 .sectionLabel}** Every employer
shall take all reasonable measures to ensure

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the health and safety of its employees and other
  individuals at a workplace under its control;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the health and safety of its employees at a workplace
  that is not under its control, to the extent that it controls their
  activities at the workplace; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the health and safety of its employees while --- and
  immediately before --- they are transported on a passenger craft.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 13, s. 45
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Specific duties

- **[[205.019]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-205.019
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every employer shall, in respect of
  each workplace under its control, and in respect of any activity
  performed by any of its employees at a workplace that is not under its
  control, to the extent that it controls the activity,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} coordinate its undertaking with the work and
    activities of the operator and of any other employer at the
    workplace who may be affected by that undertaking;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} ensure that the operator's occupational health and
    safety management system is complied with and carry out any
    responsibilities assigned to the employer under that system;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} determine, in consultation with the operator, the
    impact of its undertaking on occupational health and safety and
    ensure that other employers at the workplace who may be affected by
    that undertaking are provided with adequate information;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} communicate to its employees --- and, in respect of
    a workplace under its control, to other individuals at the workplace
    --- all information necessary to their health and safety, or ensure
    that the information is communicated to them;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} ensure that its employees comply with the
    provisions of this Part and the regulations made under this Part;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} ensure that each of its employees, and particularly
    each supervisor, is made aware of known or foreseeable health or
    safety hazards;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} ensure that its undertaking is conducted so as to
    minimize its employees' exposure to hazards and, in respect of any
    other individuals at a workplace under its control, to minimize
    their exposure to hazards;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} provide to its employees, and, in respect of a
    workplace under its control, to other individuals at the workplace,
    the facilities and personal protective equipment --- including any
    that are prescribed --- necessary for their health and safety;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} provide to its employees, and, in respect of a
    workplace under its control, to other individuals at the workplace,
    the information and training --- including any that are prescribed
    --- required for the proper use of all personal protective equipment
    that is prescribed or that is required by the operator or employer
    to be used or worn;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} provide its employees with the instruction,
    training and supervision --- including any that are prescribed ---
    necessary for their health and safety;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} ensure that the occupational health and safety
    requirements of any authorization related to the workplace are
    complied with;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} record and report to the operator all instances of
    failures to comply with the provisions of this Part or of the
    regulations made under this Part, or with the occupational health
    and safety requirements of any authorization related to the
    workplace;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} ensure that all equipment, machines, devices,
    materials and other things at the workplace are properly installed,
    stored and maintained, are safe for their intended use and are used
    as intended;

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} cooperate with and facilitate communication with
    committees established for the workplace;

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} provide to members of any special committee it
    establishes for the workplace the support, opportunities and
    training --- including any that are prescribed --- necessary to
    enable the members to fulfil the duties and functions conferred on
    the committee;

  - [(p)]{.lawlabel} ensure that all or part of the workplace as
    described in paragraphs (a) and (b) of the definition
    [[workplace]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in subsection 205.001(1) under its
    control is inspected by it or on its behalf at least once a month,
    so that every part of that workplace is inspected at least once a
    year, and ensure that the workplace committee participates; and

  - [(q)]{.lawlabel} cooperate with the Regulator and with persons
    carrying out duties or functions under this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception to paragraph (1)(p)

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite paragraph (1)(p), all or part of a
  workplace described in paragraph (a) of the definition
  [[workplace]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 205.001(1) does not
  have to be inspected at least once a month if that workplace, or that
  part of the workplace, is normally unattended and is used for the
  purpose of carrying out an offshore renewable energy project.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Training

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An employee who, with the approval of their employer,
  is receiving training that is required under this Part shall be paid
  the same wages and granted the same benefits that the employee would
  have received had they been working.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 13, s. 45
- [2024, c. 20, s. 93](#1491337-1530421){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491337-1530421.html"}
- [2024, c. 20, s. 101](#1491355-1531025){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1491355-1531025.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-205.019-20141203.html "Link to previous version of section 205.019"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Occupational health and safety program

- **[[205.02]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-205.02
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of implementing the
  operator's occupational health and safety policy, every employer
  shall, in consultation with the workplace committee, develop,
  implement and maintain, in respect of each workplace under the
  employer's control, an occupational health and safety program that
  fosters a culture of workplace safety, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} five or more employees are normally employed at the
    workplace by the employer;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the program is required by the Chief Safety
    Officer; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the requirement for such a program is prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The program shall be set out in writing and include
  provisions regarding

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the management of risks to the health and safety of
    the employees --- including any prescribed risks --- and procedures
    for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the ongoing and systematic identification and
      reporting of all hazards,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the assessment of risks associated with
      identified hazards, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the implementation of hazard control measures;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the training and supervision of the employees that
    are necessary to ensure their health and safety and that of other
    individuals at the workplace;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the establishment of special committees, the
    operation of workplace committees and special committees, the access
    by committees to a level of management with authority to resolve
    occupational health and safety matters and the information required
    under this Part to be maintained in relation to those committees;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the roles of committees and their interaction in
    implementing the operator's occupational health and safety policy;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the roles and accountability of the employers,
    employees, providers of services and suppliers that are responsible
    for implementing the operator's occupational health and safety
    policy;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the procedures, including those required under this
    Part, to be followed to protect the employees' health and safety,
    and the identification of the types of work to which those
    procedures apply;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the procedures to be followed to deal with

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} failures to comply with the program and with the
      reporting and investigating of occupational diseases, and of
      accidents, incidents and other hazardous occurrences, at the
      workplace, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the keeping of related records and statistical
      analysis;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the auditing of the adequacy and effectiveness of
    the program, including

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} determining the ability of the program to meet
      the requirements of the operator's occupational health and safety
      policy and occupational health and safety management system, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} identifying improvements that could be made to
      the program; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the implementation of the improvements identified
    during the audit referred to in paragraph (h).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the regulations establish requirements in respect
  of anything described in any of paragraphs (2)(a) to (i), the program
  shall meet the requirements of those regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2014, c. 13, s. 45
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power to require code of practice
